<!-- Document ownership registry (plans doc-ownership-lifecycle).
Identity scheme: filename minus leading YYYY-MM-DD- date prefix
and .md extension, kebab-case; flat RFC filenames without a date
prefix (*-rfc.md) take the filename minus the .md extension and
the trailing -rfc. On collision a short MMDD date suffix is
appended; when the date also collides (same base in
two completed-history directories) a directory tag (plan/backlog)
is appended too. Collided identities: 2f-paragraph-prose-lows, backlog-inbox-location-gate, check-lesson-scope-residual-evasion-classes, company-master-scoping-loop-residuals, context-budget-and-telemetry-long-running-skills, docs-branch-temp-file-hygiene, docs-branch-trap-before-restore-region, execute-plan-acceptance-witness-and-archive-closure, execute-plan-fresh-review-coverage-gaps, execute-plan-recover-stuck-done-pending-claims, learn-company-scope-placement, loop-guard-one-recorded-mutation-carve-out, maintenance-scheduler-skill, plan-readiness-trailer-gate-r5-deferrals, plans-facts-do-not-resolve-design-ambiguity, returned-for-ask-semantics, review-panel-hermeticity-dimension, review-pointer-wiring-polish, reviewed-plan-readiness-gate, rfc-design-create-mode-identity-header-wiring, sequential-landing-discipline-no-dispatch-before-squash, strengthen-jvm-review-coverage, token-usage-telemetry.; maintenance-autonomous-pipeline
Rows are backfilled; the archived column carries the actual archive date (the date the row's move executed), never the filename date prefix and never a plan-prescribed literal date. Aliases only where a genuine historical alias exists.
Table format consumed by scripts/doc_registry_validator.py. -->
